## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route from why accelerated computing, to the generative AI opportunity, products, financials and a market size, and slides 5, 13 and 22 show real craft with a labelled log chart, a plain two-column comparison and a chart paired with a takeaway. Execution is uneven, though. Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read. Many headlines are topic labels rather than claims, and the summary on slide 34 is six small lines with no visual structure, so the case has to be recovered by the reader.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Most useful repair:** Rebuild the stair-step graphics on slides 6, 7 and 33 with readable labels and sizes, and enlarge or cut the detail on slides 4, 14, 17, 19 and 20 so each diagram has one point that can be read.

**What would change the score:** I would replace topic headlines such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' with claim headlines, and make slide 34 a stronger closing page with the key figures visible.

_Confidence medium. 39 of 39 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a forward-looking statements disclaimer and a short company overview covering NVIDIA's accelerated computing platform, its installed base and developer ecosystem. It then makes the case for accelerated computing as the answer to the end of Moore's law, moving through the waves of adoption (cloud, enterprise, autonomous machines, industrial digitalization), the generative AI opportunity, data center scale workloads and return on investment. Product and go-to-market sections follow, covering DGX Cloud, NVIDIA AI Enterprise, the Grace Hopper superchip and the one-year product rhythm. Financial sections then review revenue, gross and operating margins, cash flow and capital allocation by market platform (Data Center, Gaming, Professional Visualization, Automotive), and the deck closes with a summary slide, a roughly one trillion dollar market opportunity, and appendix reconciliations of non-GAAP to GAAP figures. Slides worth studying include the platform overview on slide 4, the Moore's law chart on slide 5, the cost comparison on slide 13 and the margin chart on slide 22, where charts and single-claim headlines carry the argument.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides are in the NVIDIA investor presentation deck from October 2023?**

The deck has 39 pages in total. Slide 1 is the title slide, reading NVIDIA Investor Presentation October 2023.

**What does the NVIDIA investor presentation say about the size of the installed base and developer community?**

On slide 3, NVIDIA says its platform is installed in several hundred million computers, is available in every cloud and from every server maker, powers 74% of the TOP500 supercomputers, and boasts over 4 million developers.

**What does the NVIDIA investor presentation say about the data center infrastructure opportunity?**

Slide 6 states that the trillion dollars of installed global data center infrastructure will transition to accelerated computing to achieve an order of magnitude better performance, energy-efficiency and cost. It also says AI is driving a platform shift from general purpose to accelerated computing.

**How does NVIDIA AI Enterprise pricing work according to the NVIDIA deck?**

Slide 19 lists consumption pricing per GPU-hour and subscription pricing per GPU/year, with the subscription included with H100 PCIe and DGX. The slide describes NVIDIA AI Enterprise as the operating system for enterprise AI.

Full-bleed abstract 3D illustration of stacked, rounded, glossy green layers curving in concentric arcs from the lower right toward the upper left, resembling the NVIDIA eye motif. Light fades to white at the upper left, where thin pale green lines radiate diagonally. Composition is cropped at the right and bottom edges. ### Slide 3

**Text**

Headquarters: Santa Clara, CA
NVIDIA pioneered accelerated computing to help solve impactful
challenges classical computers cannot. A quarter of a century in the
making, NVIDIA accelerated computing is broadly recognized as the
way to advance computing as Moore’s law ends and AI lifts off.
NVIDIA’s platform is installed in several hundred million computers,
is available in every cloud and from every server maker, powers 74%
of the TOP500 supercomputers, and boasts over 4 million developers.
3
NVIDIA

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covers the whole slide edge to edge. A single dark text panel sits in the upper-left, starting 1.5% from the left edge and 5.7% from the top, spanning about 48% of the width and 37% of the height, with a thin vertical green accent bar on its left edge. Text is left-aligned with a left inset of about 5.2% of the slide width; the title sits at the top of the panel and the two body paragraphs follow below it. No separate footer; the slide number and NVIDIA logo sit together in the bottom-right corner on the photograph.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of the NVIDIA Santa Clara headquarters at golden hour. Subject: a large white-grey steel space-frame canopy with a triangular lattice roof, supported by tree-like columns, over a walkway and planters; behind it on the right is a dark glass-and-steel office building with a curved roof edge, and bare deciduous trees stand on the left and right. A low sun flares just left of centre behind the canopy, producing a bright starburst. In the foreground, a grey concrete retaining wall runs across the slide, with a strip of grass and ornamental grasses lit gold in front of it, and blurred moving cars on a road at the bottom. Composition: wide landscape framing, the canopy spanning the middle band from left edge to right of centre, the building cropped at the right edge, sky occupying the upper half. Lighting: warm low-angle sunlight with lens flare; sky is pale blue-grey fading to near white toward the horizon. Dominant colours: pale blue-grey sky, white steel, warm gold and ochre grass and light, dark charcoal glass and concrete. Not cropped except at the frame edges; full-bleed with no border and no tint or overlay on the photo itself. Also a small NVIDIA logo (green and black eye mark with NVIDIA wordmark) at bottom right, boxed by nothing, over the grass area.

**Design**

Background is a photograph with a pale grey-white base (#F2F2F2 and #FFFFFF fills underneath). Dark panel is charcoal #262626 at 85% opacity, square corners, no border, no shadow. Accent bar is NVIDIA green #76B900, flush with the top-left corner. Title colour NVIDIA green #76B900; body text white #FFFFFF; slide number black #000000. Typeface: NVIDIASans, a humanist geometric sans-serif (closest common match Helvetica-like sans-serif). Title in Medium weight, body in Regular weight. Title size about 4.1% of slide height (cap height roughly 2.9%); body text about 2.2% of slide height, set with tight leading (line pitch about 2.4% of slide height). Shapes have sharp corners, no gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read.

NVIDIA’s Accelerated Computing Platform

With nearly three decades of a singular focus, NVIDIA is expert at accelerating software and scaling compute by a Million-X, going well beyond Moore’s law.
Accelerated computing requires full-stack innovation – optimizing across every layer of computing – from silicon and systems to software and algorithms, demanding deep understanding of the problem domain.
Our full-stack platforms – NVIDIA HPC, NVIDIA AI, and NVIDIA Omniverse – accelerate high performance computing, AI, and industrial digitalization workloads.
We accelerate workloads at data center scale, across thousands of compute nodes, treating the network and storage as part of the computing fabric.
Our platform extends from the cloud and enterprise data centers to supercomputing centers, edge computing and PCs.
4

**Layout**

Single-slide layout on a white field with a centred title and green subtitle across the top. Left two-thirds hold a diagram stack: an AI application framework row of 14 tiles at the top, a row of three platform circles with labels, a grey library panel, and a grey cloud-to-edge panel with three chip icons; labels sit in a right-aligned column at about x 16% on the left. The right third (from about x 70%) holds a left-aligned five-paragraph body text block, top-aligned with the framework row. Page number 4 and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom right, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram. (1) AI application framework: 14 square tiles in one horizontal row, each about 4.0% wide and 7.1% high, spaced about 4.3% apart, each a small framework render with its name in white capitals at the bottom: MODULUS (colourful heat-map grid on dark navy), MONAI (dark green rendered sphere), RIVA (figure with a cyan glowing shape), MAXINE (video call window with a face), NEMO (blue droplet character with eyes), MERLIN (white and blue device rendering), CUOPT (street scene with a wheeled robot), MORPHEUS (dark hexagon grid with orange dots), TOKKIO (purple-haired digital human on dark background), AVATAR (digital human figure with raised hand), DRIVE (street view with a car on a road), ISAAC (white robotic arm on grey), METROPOLIS (red and dark city-scale rendering with coloured labels), HOLOSCAN (blue-brown dotted terrain rendering). (2) Three bright green #76B900 filled circles, each about 4.0% wide, with a white line icon: an atom-like orbit icon for NVIDIA HPC at left, a network-of-nodes icon for NVIDIA AI in the centre, and concentric swirl rings for NVIDIA Omniverse at right; each has its label to the right. Thin grey straight lines fan from the bottom edge of the framework tiles down to the three circles. (3) Acceleration libraries panel: a light grey rounded rectangle holding 17 pill-shaped light grey chips in three rows, row one with 6 chips (cuNumeric, CV-CUDA, cuQuantum, Parabricks, Sionna, Jetpack), row two with 8 chips (RAPIDS, Spark, cuDNN, cuGraph, TensorRT, Triton, Deepstream, Flare), row three with 3 centred chips (DOCA, Mag IO, Aerial). (4) Cloud-to-edge panel: a light grey rounded rectangle holding a row of 8 round-cornered grey chips (RTX, DGX, HGX, EGX, OVX, SuperPOD with the label on two lines, AGX, IGX). Thin grey lines run from the bottom of each chip down to three green circles below, each with a white line icon: a grid of squares for GPU at left, a four-square grid for CPU in the centre, and a dense dot matrix for DPU at right, labelled GPU, CPU and DPU beneath. (5) Small NVIDIA logo, green wordmark with eye symbol, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no gradient, texture or shadows. Title is bold NVIDIA Sans-style geometric sans-serif in black #000000, about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Subtitle is regular NVIDIA Sans-style sans-serif in green #76B900, about 2.96%, centred. Body text is regular sans-serif in black #000000 about 2.22%, with bold words in the same colour. Section labels are medium-weight sans-serif capitals in black #000000 about 1.73%. Chip and platform labels are regular sans-serif in black about 1.23% and 1.48%. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the subtitle, circles and the right edge bar. Panels are #F2F2F2 with rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. Chips are #C8C8C8 with rounded corners, no outline, no shadow. Connector lines are thin mid-grey #A0A0A0 strokes, about 0.5 pt, no arrowheads.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A log-scale chart with a short quote header makes the post-Moore's law argument in one visual.
- Review: The deck has a clear route from why accelerated computing, to the generative AI opportunity, products, financials and a market size, and slides 5, 13 and 22 show real craft with a labelled log chart, a plain two-column comparison and a chart paired with a takeaway.
- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

Why Accelerated Computing?
Advancing computing in the post-Moore’s Law era
Accelerated computing is needed to tackle the most
impactful opportunities of our time—like AI, climate
simulation, drug discovery, ray tracing, and robotics.
NVIDIA is uniquely dedicated to accelerated computing
—working top-to-bottom—refactoring applications and
creating new algorithms, and bottom-to-top—inventing
new specialized processors, like RT Core and Tensor Core.
“It’s the end of Moore’s Law as we know it.”
- John Hennessy, Oct 2018
“Moore’s Law is dead.”
- Jensen Huang, GTC 2013

**Layout**

Two-column slide: a white left column (text from about 7% to 47% of width) and a light grey right panel (#F2F2F2) starting at 52.9% of width and running full height, holding the chart. The title sits top-left at about 13% from the top, with the green subtitle directly beneath it, a thin grey horizontal rule at about 31% height spanning the left column, then the body text, two quotes with attributions, all left-aligned to a 7.4% margin. The footer row at about 95% height holds the NVIDIA logo at bottom-left and the page number 5 beside it; a green vertical bar sits at the far bottom-left edge.

**Visuals**

Chart: a scatter-and-line chart with a logarithmic vertical axis titled Trillions of Operations per Second (TOPS), with tick labels 10^2, 10^3, 10^4, 10^5, 10^6, 10^7, 10^8 and 10^9 (no units beyond TOPS printed). The horizontal axis shows years 1980, 1990, 2000, 2010, 2020 and 2030 with no axis title; the baseline is a thin grey line running from about 1980 to 2030. Series 1, GPU-Computing perf (green #76B900): a straight line with about 9 markers from about 2008 (about 10^5.2) to about 2022 (about 10^8.7), with a dashed continuation to about 2024 (about 10^9); labelled 2X per year. Series 2, Single-threaded CPU perf (blue #0070C0): about 60 scattered dots from about 1990 (about 10^2.2) to about 2017 (about 10^4.2), with a solid blue trend line rising from about 1990 to about 2017 and a flattening dashed blue continuation to about 2025 near 10^4.2; labelled 1.1X per year at the flattened end and 1.5X perf per year on the middle slope. Annotation: a vertical double-headed thin black arrow at about 2024 spanning about 10^5 to about 10^8, labelled 1000X / In 10 years. No legend. Highlighted points: none beyond the green end markers.

**Design**

Background: Split solid background: white #FFFFFF on the left and light grey #F2F2F2 on the right panel from 52.9% of width to the right edge. Clean corporate NVIDIA style. Background white #FFFFFF on the left, light grey #F2F2F2 panel on the right. Title black #000000 in NVIDIA Sans Bold (a geometric-humanist sans-serif, bold weight), about 4.45% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%). Subtitle green #76B900 in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.96% of height. Body text black #000000 in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.72% of height, roughly 1.4 line spacing. Quotes in NVIDIA Sans Italic, green #76B900, about 2.72% of height; attributions in NVIDIA Sans Regular black, about 1.98%. Chart labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 1.73% of height (tick labels about 1.48%). Accent colours: green #76B900 for GPU series and subtitle, blue #0070C0 for CPU series and annotations. Rule line in mid grey #8C8C8C, about 0.15% stroke, square ends, no shadow. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild the stair-step graphics on slides 6, 7 and 33 with readable labels and sizes, and enlarge or cut the detail on slides 4, 14, 17, 19 and 20 so each diagram has one point that can be read.

**Text**

Waves of Adoption of Accelerated Computing
A generational computing platform shift
Industrial Digitalization
Autonomous Vehicles & Robotics
Enterprise
Cloud Service Providers & Consumer Internet
A new computing era has begun.
Accelerated computing enabled the rise of AI, which is driving a platform shift from general purpose to accelerated computing, and enabling new, never-before-possible applications.
The trillion dollars of installed global data center infrastructure will transition to accelerated computing to achieve an order of magnitude better performance, energy-efficiency and cost.
Hyperscale cloud service providers and consumer internet companies have been the early adopters of AI and accelerated computing, with broader enterprise adoption now under way.
AI and accelerated computing will also make possible the next big waves – autonomous machines and industrial digitalization.
6

**Layout**

A centred title block sits at the top with the title over a green subtitle. Below it, on the left, four staggered bold labels step diagonally from upper right to lower left as a wave progression, and on the right a single text column of body paragraphs runs from the upper-middle to the lower-right. The page number and NVIDIA logo sit in the bottom-right corner, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram-like arrangement of four bold text labels with no drawn shapes or arrows: Industrial Digitalization (top), Autonomous Vehicles & Robotics, Enterprise, Cloud Service Providers & Consumer Internet (bottom-left). A small green wave-like icon (three curved strokes, approximately ≈ shape) sits at the lower-left near x 6.5%, y 90%. Bottom-right NVIDIA logo in green (#76B900) with black wordmark, and a thin vertical green bar at the far right edge. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title text black (#000000) in NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.45% of slide height for the title, sans-serif, tightly spaced. Subtitle in NVIDIA green (#76B900), NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.96% of slide height. Body text black (#000000) in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.22% of slide height, left-aligned with generous line spacing. Wave labels are NVIDIASans Bold, about 1.73% of slide height, black. Accent colour green #76B900 used for subtitle, wave icon, logo and right edge bar. No shadows, gradients, or boxes; no visible rules.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild the stair-step graphics on slides 6, 7 and 33 with readable labels and sizes, and enlarge or cut the detail on slides 4, 14, 17, 19 and 20 so each diagram has one point that can be read.

**Text**

NVIDIA Accelerated Computing for Every Wave / Industrial Digitalization / NVIDIA Omniverse is a software platform for designing, / building, and operating 3D and virtual world simulations. / It harnesses the power of NVIDIA graphics and AI technologies / and runs on NVIDIA-powered data centers and workstations. / Autonomous Vehicles / & Robotics / NVIDIA DRIVE is a full-stack platform for autonomous vehicles (AV) that / includes hardware for in-car compute, such as the Orin system-on-chip, / and the full AV and AI cockpit software stack. / Enterprise / NVIDIA DGX Cloud is a cloud service that allows enterprises immediate access to / the infrastructure and software needed to train advanced models for generative AI / and other groundbreaking applications. / NVIDIA AI Enterprise is the operating system of AI, with enterprise-grade security, stability, / manageability and support. It is available on all major CSPs and server OEMs and supports / enterprise deployment of AI in production. / Cloud Service Providers / & Consumer Internet / NVIDIA HGX is an AI supercomputing platform purpose-built for AI. It includes 8 NVIDIA GPUs, as well as / interconnect and networking technologies, delivering order-of-magnitude performance speed-ups for AI / over CPU servers. It is broadly available from all major server OEMs/ODMs. NVIDIA DGX, an AI server based / on the same architecture, along with NVIDIA AI software and support, is also available. / 7

**Layout**

Title is centred in the top band at about 7% from the top. Below it, the content is a descending staircase of five rows: each row has a bold black category label on the left and a text block to its right, stepping rightward and downward from Industrial Digitalization (top) to Cloud Service Providers (bottom). The left third of the slide is empty apart from a small green icon at lower left. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with the page number 7 to its left and a thin green bar at the far right edge; margins are about 1 to 8 percent, and body text is justified.

**Visuals**

Three non-photographic graphics only, no charts, tables, or photographs. (1) A small green decorative squiggle icon at lower left, about 1.5 by 2 percent of the slide, roughly two stacked wavy strokes like the letter S pair, in #76B900 with thin stroke. (2) The NVIDIA wordmark logo at bottom right, black text 'NVIDIA' with the green-and-black eye symbol to its left, placed unboxed on the white background. (3) A vertical solid green bar at the far right edge of the bottom of the slide, #76B900, flush to the right edge and bottom, about 0.4 percent wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black and NVIDIA green (#76B900) accents; no boxes, no shadows, no gradients, no corner radii. Titles and category labels are black #000000 NVIDIASans Bold (a geometric humanist sans-serif, bold weight), title about 4.45 percent of slide height. Body text is NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 1.98 percent of slide height, justified. Product names are set in NVIDIASans Bold in green #76B900 at the start of each paragraph. Category labels are about 1.73 percent of slide height. Page number is NVIDIASans Regular about 1.48 percent.

### Slide 8

**Text**

NVIDIA’s Accelerated Computing Ecosystem / The NVIDIA accelerated computing platform has / attracted the largest ecosystem of developers, / supporting a rapidly growing universe of / applications and industry innovation. / Developers can engage with NVIDIA through / CUDA – our parallel computing programming / model introduced in 2006 – or at higher layers / of the stack, including libraries, pre-trained AI / models, SDKs and other development tools. / 300 Libraries / 400 AI Models / 100 Updated in the Last Year / Developers / 1.8M / 4M / 2020 / 2023 / CUDA Downloads* / 20M / 45M / 2020 / 2023 / *Cumulative / AI Startups / 6K / 15K / 2020 / 2023 / GPU-Accelerated Applications / 700 / 3,200 / 2020 / 2023 / 8

**Layout**

Single slide with a centred title across the top, a two-column body: a left text column (intro paragraphs, a stat block, a horizontal rule and a caption) and a right 2x2 grid of four equal grey panels. Left column text is left-aligned at about 9.9% of slide width; the stat block and caption are centred on about 25% of width. Page number 8 and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom right, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Four bubble-chart panels in a 2x2 grid, each a grey rounded-free rectangle with a light grey border, containing two green circles sized by value on a shared baseline with year labels beneath. Top-left panel: Developers; circles 1.8M (2020, small) and 4M (2023, large). Top-right panel: CUDA Downloads* (cumulative); circles 20M (2020) and 45M (2023). Bottom-left panel: AI Startups; circles 6K (2020, small) and 15K (2023, large). Bottom-right panel: GPU-Accelerated Applications; circles 700 (2020, smallest) and 3,200 (2023, largest). Each panel has a thin grey baseline across the lower part with years 2020 and 2023 beneath. Circle area is proportional to value. The NVIDIA eye logo with wordmark sits bottom right at about 93% x, 95% y.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles and body text black #000000. Accent colour NVIDIA green #76B900 used for all bubble circles and the vertical bar at the right edge. Panels fill light grey #EBEBEB with a thin border about #C8C8C8. Typeface NVIDIASans, a clean geometric humanist sans-serif. Slide title bold, about 4.45% of slide height. Body paragraphs regular about 2.72% of slide height. Stat numerals medium about 3.71%, labels light about 2.96%. Panel titles and circle values medium about 2.5%. Year labels about 1.5%. Rule line thin black or dark grey. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a circular flywheel diagram with its caption occupies the left column (about x 5 to 42 percent), and a text block with four bulleted items occupies the right column starting at about x 52 percent. The title is centred across the top at about y 7 percent, with no subtitle. The bottom right corner carries the page number 9 and the NVIDIA logo, with a thin green bar at the far right edge. Left margin is about 5 percent and right margin about 4 percent; body text is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

One diagram: a circular virtuous-cycle flywheel drawn with thin black (#000000, about 0.1 percent of slide height) curved arrows with small open arrowheads, no fills. Nodes are plain black text labels with no boxes: Scale (top centre, about x 23 y 27.5), R&D $ (below Scale, about x 23 y 34), Speed-Up (below R&D $, about x 22 y 40.6), Installed Base (left, about x 5 y 37.5), Developers (bottom left, about x 6 y 63), Cloud & OEMs (bottom right, about x 37 y 63), End-Users (bottom centre, about x 22 y 81). Connections: short straight downward arrows link Scale to R&D $ and R&D $ to Speed-Up. A curved arrow runs from Speed-Up down and left to Developers. A curved arrow runs from Installed Base down to Developers. A curved arrow runs from Developers down and right to End-Users. A curved arrow runs from End-Users up and right to Cloud & OEMs. A curved arrow runs from Cloud & OEMs up along the right side to Scale, with its arrowhead pointing left into Scale. A large outer arc runs from Cloud & OEMs up over the top of the diagram and down-left to Installed Base, with its arrowhead at Installed Base. The caption below the diagram is set in NVIDIASans Medium. The NVIDIA logo at bottom right is an icon with a green eye mark (#76B900) and a black NVIDIA wordmark, about 5.5 by 3.3 percent, unboxed, on the white background. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or shadow. Title and body text are black #000000. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900, used for the bullet dots, the logo and the thin bar at the right edge. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans, close to Helvetica or Arial): Bold for the title and bold lead-ins of bullets, Regular for body and diagram labels, Medium for the caption. **Text**

Huge ROI from AI Driving a Powerful New Investment Cycle
AI can augment creativity and productivity by orders of magnitude across industries
Knowledge workers will use copilots based on large
language models to generate documents, answer
questions, or summarize missed meetings, emails
and chats – adding hours of productivity per week.
Copilots specialized for fields such as software
development, legal services or education can boost
productivity by as much as 50%.
Social media, search and e-commerce apps are
using deep recommenders to offer more relevant
content and ads to their customers, increasing
engagement and monetization.
Creators can generate stunning, photorealistic
images with a single text prompt – compressing
workflows that take days or weeks into minutes in
industries from advertising to game development.
Call center agents augmented with AI chatbots
can dramatically increase productivity and
customer satisfaction.
Drug discovery, financial services, agriculture and
food services and climate forecasting are seeing
order-of-magnitude workflow acceleration from AI.

**Layout**

Centered two-line header at the top (black bold title above a green subtitle), a left text column of body copy about 15% to 41% of the width, and a three-by-three grid of image tiles with captions on the right, from about 44% to 93% of the width. Columns of tiles are at x 44.4, 60.9 and 77.4 percent, rows at y 23.6, 47.5 and 71.3 percent, each image 15.5 by 15.7 percent with captions beneath. A small italic source line sits bottom right of centre, followed by the page number 10 and the NVIDIA logo at the bottom right, with a thin green bar at the far right edge. Margins are about 7% left and about 1% right of the grid.

**Visuals**

Nine photographic or illustrative tiles arranged in a 3x3 grid, each boxed as a flat rectangle with square corners and no border. Row 1: (a) Office AI Copilots, a dark screenshot of the Windows Start menu showing an app list on the left (Media Player, Microsoft Edge, Microsoft Endpoint Manager, Microsoft Intune Management Ext..., Microsoft Office Tools expanded with Database Compare, Office Language Preferences, Spreadsheet Compare, Telemetry Log for Office) and tiles on the right (Microsoft Edge, Maps, Weather, Play and explore, Xbox Console, Movies & TV) in grey and charcoal. (b) Search & Social Media, a close photograph of a person's hands holding a black tablet showing a shopping website with a pink banner and product images of shoes, warm natural light, shallow depth of field. (c) AI Content Creation, a photograph of a dark car drawn with a wireframe sketch overlay on a desert dusk landscape with orange and grey tones, plus a small white inset card reading 'A beautiful desert sky late in the evening.' and a small desert thumbnail at its upper right. Row 2: (d) Legal Services, Education, a bright classroom or office photograph with a presenter standing at left by a wall screen showing green slides, and seated people with laptops at tables in the foreground, white walls, cool daylight. (e) AI Software Development, a flat dark charcoal #2F2F2F panel with a line diagram of two stacked-block server icons, one on the left and one on the right, grey rectangles inside them, joined by a connecting line with small circular nodes below. (f) Financial Services, a dark photograph of stock market screens with red and green numbers and tickers over glowing green upward arrows. Row 3: (g) Customer Service with AI, a photograph of a woman wearing a headset looking at a wall-mounted monitor displaying three panels of interface windows with green buttons, cool grey tones. (h) Drug Discovery, a dark grid of chemical structure diagrams (molecular skeletons with coloured atoms) on black, some cells dimmed. (i) Agri-Food | Climate, a close photograph of young green maize seedlings in brown soil with a fine water spray and a red-and-grey device at the top, warm earth tones. Also an NVIDIA logo in green at bottom right and a thin vertical green bar at the far right edge.

### Slide 11

**Text**

Generative AI / The most important computing platform of our generation / The era of generative AI has arrived, / unlocking new opportunities for AI across / many different applications. / Generative AI is trained on large amounts / of data to find patterns and relationships, / learning the representation of almost / anything with structure. / It can then be prompted to generate text, / images, video, code, or even proteins. / For the very first time, computers can / augment the human ability to generate / information and create. / 1,600+ Generative AI companies are / building on NVIDIA. / TEXT / AUDIO / IMAGE / 3D / VIDEO / DNA / PROTEIN / MOLECULE / ANIMATION / ANIMATION / MOLECULE / PROTEIN / DNA / VIDEO / 3D / IMAGE / AUDIO / TEXT / 11

**Layout**

Centred title and green subtitle sit across the top of the slide, with a symmetrical two-column diagram on the left-centre and a left-aligned text column on the right. Left column labels are right-aligned beside their thumbnails, right column labels are left-aligned, the central network icon sits between the two thumbnail columns, and the page number and NVIDIA logo sit in the bottom-right footer, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a symmetrical flow. Ten-row left column of thumbnails (TEXT, AUDIO, IMAGE, 3D, VIDEO, DNA, PROTEIN, MOLECULE, ANIMATION, top to bottom) and a mirrored right column (ANIMATION, MOLECULE, PROTEIN, DNA, VIDEO, 3D, IMAGE, AUDIO, TEXT, top to bottom). Each thumbnail is a small landscape rectangle, about 5 percent of slide width, with a thin grey-blue border. Thin grey curved lines run from each left thumbnail to a single convergence point at about x 41 y 52 on the left side of a central icon, and from the same point to each right thumbnail. Left thumbnails: TEXT shows a white card with a green ChatGPT-style logo and the text 'Quantum co' / 'mechanics to' cut off at the right edge; AUDIO shows a black field with a bright green audio waveform; IMAGE shows a red, green and blue macaw parrot on a pale blue background; 3D shows a black-background wireframe colosseum model with green glow; VIDEO shows a purple jellyfish on a dark blue background; DNA shows a green double helix on black; PROTEIN shows pink and yellow ribbon protein structure on black; MOLECULE shows a grey and green ball-and-stick molecule on dark blue; ANIMATION shows a cartoon girl at a desk with glowing green and yellow screens, warm tones. Right thumbnails repeat the same images in mirrored order. Central icon: two grey-blue (#6B7394) stacked-block neural network symbols, an upper row of vertical bars, trapezoid encoder and decoder shapes, and a lower row of stacked rectangles connected by a grey elbow line with small circle nodes at the bottom. Lighting is flat and the thumbnails are not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. Title black #000000 in bold NVIDIA Sans, about 4.5 percent of slide height, centred. Subtitle in green #76B900 regular NVIDIA Sans, about 3 percent of slide height, centred. Body text black #000000 regular NVIDIA Sans, about 2.7 percent of slide height, left-aligned with paragraph spacing. Closing sentence in green #76B900 regular. Diagram labels black #000000 regular NVIDIA Sans, about 1.2 percent of slide height, all caps. Thin grey #A0A8B8 connector lines, about 0.1 percent of slide height wide. Thumbnails have thin grey-blue borders #8A90A0 and square corners. Page number black. Green accent bar #76B900 at right edge.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Modern AI is a Data Center Scale Computing Workload
Data centers are becoming AI factories: Data as input, intelligence as output
Before Transformers = 8x / 2yrs
Transformers = 215x / 2yrs

2011 2012 2013 2014 2015 2016 2017 2018 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023
AI Training Computational Requirements
Fueling Giant-Scale AI Infrastructure
NVIDIA compute & networking GPU | DPU | CPU
Large Language Models, based on the Transformer architecture,
are one of today’s most important advanced AI technologies,
involving up to trillions of parameters that learn from text.
Developing them is an expensive, time-consuming process that
demands deep technical expertise, distributed data center-scale
infrastructure, and a full-stack accelerated computing approach.
12

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white 16:9 slide: a bold centred title with a green centred subtitle across the top, then a left chart panel and a right photograph panel of equal height (about 44.7 percent of slide width each) sitting side by side with a gap of roughly 6 percent between them, each with a centred caption below. Left margin about 4.5 percent, right margin about 4.5 percent; page number '12' and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom right, and a thin green vertical bar sits at the far right edge at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Chart (left panel): log-scale scatter chart with two straight trend lines, titled AI Training Computational Requirements. Y axis: 'Training Compute (petaFLOPs)', logarithmic from 10^2 at bottom to 10^10 at top, decade gridlines labelled 10^2, 10^3, 10^4, 10^5, 10^6, 10^7, 10^8, 10^9, 10^10. X axis: years 2011 to 2023 labelled at integer years, no unit, axis line dark grey. Series 1 (blue #4472C4 dots and straight line): labelled points about AlexNet 2012 near 10^2.6, Seq2Seq and Resnet about 2014 near 10^4.2, VGG-19 about 2014 near 10^4.1, ResNeXt about 2016.4 near 10^4.1, DenseNet201 about 2016.4 near 10^3.4, Xception about 2015.5 near 10^5.7, InceptionV3 about 2015.4 near 10^5.2, ELMo about 2017.9 near 10^3.6, MoCo ResNet50 about 2019.6 near 10^6.0, Wav2Vec 2.0 about 2019.7 near 10^6.2; blue line runs from about 2011.6 at 10^2.6 to about 2019.8 at 10^6.1. Series 2 (green #70AD47 dots and straight line): Transformer about 2017.5 near 10^4.4, GPT-1 about 2018.0 near 10^5.0, BERT Large about 2018.4 near 10^5.6, XLNet about 2018.9 near 10^6.6, Megatron-NLG about 2019.0 near 10^7.0, GPT-2 about 2018.9 near 10^7.2, Microsoft T-NLG about 2019.9 near 10^7.5, GPT-3 about 2020.0 near 10^8.8, BLOOM about 2022.3 near 10^8.9, Chinchilla about 2022.3 near 10^9.0, MT NLG 530B about 2021.4 near 10^9.1, PaLM about 2021.8 near 10^9.5; green line runs from about 2017.5 at 10^4.4 to about 2021.8 at 10^9.5. Values are estimates read from the plot positions, not printed on the slide. Legend: none; instead two coloured annotation lines at top left: blue text Before Transformers = 8x / 2yrs and green text Transformers = 215x / 2yrs. Highlighted points: none. Right panel: photograph of a dark data centre hall (see elements).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF; title and body text black #000000; subtitle and accent green #76B900; chart series blue #4472C4 and green #70AD47; photo panel and overlay near-black #151617. Typeface NVIDIASans (geometric humanist sans-serif): title Bold, subtitle Regular, captions Medium, chart labels Regular. Title size about 4.45 percent of slide height, subtitle about 2.96 percent, captions about 2.47 percent, body text on photo about 1.98 percent, chart labels about 1.23 percent, axis labels about 1.48 percent. Chart frame: thin light grey #BFBFBF 0.75 pt rectangle, square corners, no shadow. Photo: sharp rectangle, no corner radius, no shadow.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Side-by-side server racks and a cost and energy callout turn a technical comparison into a single clear claim.
- Review: The deck has a clear route from why accelerated computing, to the generative AI opportunity, products, financials and a market size, and slides 5, 13 and 22 show real craft with a labelled log chart, a plain two-column comparison and a chart paired with a takeaway.
- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

Full-Stack & Data Center Scale Acceleration
Drive significant cost savings and workload scaling
Classical Computing — 960 CPU-only servers
Application
CPU server racks
Accelerated Computing — 2 GPU servers
Application
Re-Engineered for Acceleration
CUDA-X Acceleration Libraries
Magnum IO
25X lower cost
84X better energy-efficiency
LLM Workload: Bert-Large Training and Inference | CPU Server: Dual-EYPC 7763 | GPU Server: Dual-EPYC 7763 + 8X H100 PCIe GPUs
13

**Layout**

Two equal-width bordered panels sit side by side in the middle band of the slide, left panel at x 4.5 to 49.3 percent and right panel at x 50.8 to 95.3 percent, both spanning y 23.6 to 81.8 percent. The centred title sits at the top, with a green centred subtitle beneath it, and each panel heading is centred at the top of its panel. A small italic footnote is left-aligned at the bottom left below the panels, and the page number 13 sits at the bottom right beside the NVIDIA logo, with a green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Two-panel comparison diagram built from rectangles and icons. Left panel: a full-width green rounded rectangle labelled 'Application' stacked above a light grey rounded rectangle labelled 'CPU server racks' that contains five identical line-art server rack icons in a row, each drawn in green #76B900 outline with small dash marks on each shelf. Right panel: three stacked green rounded rectangles labelled 'Application / Re-Engineered for Acceleration', 'CUDA-X Acceleration Libraries' and 'Magnum IO', followed by one green outline icon of a GPU card centred below them, and below the icon the bold text '25X lower cost' and '84X better energy-efficiency'. No chart, table or photograph. Connections: none; the stacks are vertical with no arrows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold black NVIDIA Sans sans-serif, about 4.45 percent of slide height. Subtitle in regular NVIDIA Sans, green #76B900, about 2.96 percent. Panel headings in NVIDIA Sans, first word medium/bold and rest regular, black #000000, about 2.72 percent. Body labels in regular black #000000 about 2.22 percent. Stat lines in medium black about 2.47 percent. Accent colour green #76B900 for subtitle, all filled bars, rack and GPU icons, and the logo. Panels have thin light grey #C8C8C8 hairline borders with square corners and no shadows. Green bars have fully rounded corners. The left CPU rack container is light grey #EBEBEB with rounded corners. Footnote in italic grey #8C8C8C about 1.23 percent.

**Layout**

Single slide with a centred bold title across the top, two pie charts on a common horizontal band in the middle, the left pie small and the right pie large and vertically centred near the middle, linked by a faint translucent wedge; the left pie has its flat edge at x 52.3% and the right pie sits at x 57.9% to 82.5%. The slide number 14 sits bottom right at x 91.3%, followed by the NVIDIA logo at x 93.1% and a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge; left margin about 8.9% and the left-hand label starts at x 8.9%.

**Visuals**

Two pie charts linked by a translucent connector wedge. Left pie titled 4-Year Cost of AI Infrastructure, ~$1B, with the subtitle 16K GPU above it: a circle cut vertically so only the right half is a full slice set; slices are DC Facility Build and Operate (light grey #CDCDCD, about 50 percent, left half), GPU Compute (green #76B900, about 40 percent, upper right), and Networking (teal #008564, about 10 percent, lower right). Right pie titled 4-Year Rental Opportunity, @$4 per GPU-HR, ~$2.5B, a full circle with slices 25 percent Performance Increase Worth $600M+ (light grey #B8B8B8, lower right quarter), 15% Utilization Increase Worth $350M+ (dark grey #8C8C8C, lower left about 15 percent), and the large remaining blue #0071C5 slice (about 60 percent, upper and left) with no printed value. Two curved double-headed black arrows, one on the blue slice upper right near the boundary with the light grey slice and one at the lower left boundary between blue and dark grey. Thin black leader lines with small filled black dots end at each slice label. Connector: a faint light grey #CDCDCD translucent area with dashed hairline edges runs from the top of the left pie's green/grey boundary to the top-left of the blue slice and from the bottom of the left pie to the bottom of the dark grey slice. No legend; labels sit beside the slices.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. Title is black #000000 NVIDIASans Bold, sans-serif, about 4.45 percent of slide height, centred. Chart labels are black #000000 NVIDIASans Medium for headings (about 2.47 percent of slide height) and NVIDIASans Regular for body labels (about 1.98 percent). Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900 and teal #008564 in the left pie, blue #0071C5 in the right pie, greys #CDCDCD #B8B8B8 #8C8C8C. Slices have thin white #FFFFFF separators. No corner radii or shadows. Slide number and logo are small at the bottom right, with a green #76B900 bar at the right edge.

### Slide 15

**Text**

NVIDIA Go-to-Market Across Cloud and On-Premises / Reaching customers everywhere / CLOUD / ON-PREM / DGX Cloud / Google Cloud / Microsoft Azure / ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure / NVIDIA AI Foundations / DGX / PARTNERS / aws / Google Cloud / Microsoft Azure / ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure / DELL Technologies / Hewlett Packard Enterprise / Lenovo / HGX / INFERENCE / MGX / AGX / IGX / 15

**Layout**

Two equal columns of light grey panels sit side by side under a centred two-line title block, with the CLOUD and ON-PREM column headers centred above each panel. A row label column sits at the far left, with the NVIDIA logo at upper left and the PARTNERS label aligned to the lower rows. The title is centred at the top, the page number and NVIDIA logo sit in the bottom-right footer, and a short green bar sits on the right edge.

**Visuals**

Two rectangular diagrams-in-panels, no charts. Left CLOUD panel contains: a black-outlined cloud icon labelled DGX Cloud with the logos Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure and ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure inside it, captioned NVIDIA AI Foundations; a row of four partner logos (aws, Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure, ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure); and two black line icons labelled HGX (a rack server unit with vents and horizontal slots) and INFERENCE (a stacked tapered cone of layered slabs with a vertical grid edge). Right ON-PREM panel contains: a black line icon of a DGX box labelled DGX; a row of three partner logos (DELL Technologies, Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Lenovo); and three black line icons labelled MGX (a flat rectangle with rounded inner slot), AGX (a rounded-corner square module with a small circle at bottom right) and IGX (a rectangle filled with diagonal hatching). Icons are monochrome black outline drawings with no shading.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with black NVIDIA sans-serif type and a single green accent. Title is bold black #000000, about 4.5 percent of slide height. Subtitle is regular #76B900 green, about 3.0 percent. Column headers CLOUD and ON-PREM are medium black #000000 at about 2.5 percent. Body labels are regular black #000000 at about 2.0 percent; small cloud caption about 1.5 percent. Grey panels are flat #EBEBEB with thin #D0D0D0 hairline edges and square corners, no shadow. Icons are black #000000 line art about 2 px stroke. Green accent bar at right edge #76B900.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read.

**Text**

Training & Inference — One Architecture
Cloud | On-Prem | Edge
TRAINING
NVIDIA DGX | HGX H100
NVIDIA L40S
INFERENCE
IN THE DATA CENTER
NVIDIA L40
Image Generation
NVIDIA L4
AI Video
NVIDIA H100 | L40S
Universal GPUs
NVIDIA Grace Hopper
RecSys, Gen AI
AT THE EDGE
IGX
Industrial-Grade System
for Healthcare, Logistics,
Manufacturing
AGX
Functionally-Safe System
for Autonomous Vehicles
16

**Layout**

Three-column arrangement centred on the slide: training photograph at left (x 4.8 to 36.6%), a circular-arrow diagram in the centre (x 40.8 to 59.2%), and two grey inference panels at right (x 63.4 to 95.3%). The title is centred at the top (y 7.3%) with a green subtitle beneath it; the TRAINING and INFERENCE column headings sit at y 31.3%, above the visuals. Margins are about 5% left and right. The page number 16 and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom right at y 95 to 98%, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Left: a photograph of NVIDIA DGX H100 server racks, full colour rectangle, not cropped, boxed with hard edges. Subject: about nine tall black server racks in a row receding in perspective, the front racks with beige-gold panels, the rightmost racks darker with mesh vents. Composition: landscape, slightly low three-quarter view, racks fill the lower two-thirds of the frame over a glossy black reflective floor. Lighting: studio spotlight on the racks against a black backdrop. Dominant colours: black, charcoal and tan/gold. White label text overlaid at the top centre of the photo. Centre: a line illustration of two circular arrows, one arc over the top with arrowhead at lower left and one arc below with arrowhead at upper right, in dark grey #4D4D4D, about 2px stroke, enclosing a diagram of two server-like stacks: a smaller rectangular outline at left (stacked horizontal bars with two small circles and a dial symbol at its base) and a taller rectangular outline at right (stacked horizontal bars with two circles and dial symbols at its base), joined by an orthogonal line that runs from the top of the left stack, across and down to the middle of the right stack. Right: two light grey panels (#EBEBEB) with no border and square corners, each containing centred black text; no icons or logos inside them.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Rebuild the stair-step graphics on slides 6, 7 and 33 with readable labels and sizes, and enlarge or cut the detail on slides 4, 14, 17, 19 and 20 so each diagram has one point that can be read.

**Text**

NVIDIA AI Enterprise / Enterprise is the Next Big Generative AI Opportunity / NVIDIA / AI / Playground / DGX Cloud / Microsoft Azure / Google Cloud / ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure / Enterprise On-Prem / DELL / Hewlett Packard Enterprise / Lenovo / vmware / AI Chatbot / “RAG” / Vector DB / Cloud AI / APIs / NVIDIA / AI / Foundations / NVIDIA / AI / Enterprise / LLM Model / Container / Enterprise SaaS / & AI Platforms / Adobe / databricks / gettyimages / Hugging Face / servicenow / snowflake / WPP / DGX Cloud / Microsoft Azure / Google Cloud / ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure / Cloud AI / APIs / 17 / Enterprise AI Chatbots are built as Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) workflows, which augment the knowledge in the / LLM with vectorized Enterprise data. These Chatbots serve as apprentices, improving the productivity of every employee in / every Enterprise company. / NVIDIA delivers this capability to Enterprises by packaging LLMs with NVIDIA AI Enterprise, the runtime for hosting the / LLMs, into containers that can be deployed anywhere – on any cloud, on premises, or within Enterprise SaaS applications.

**Layout**

Centred title block at top with the title and green subtitle centred horizontally; a left-to-right flow diagram occupies the middle band, with the NVIDIA AI Playground, NVIDIA AI Foundations and DGX Cloud stacked in a left column, the LLM Model Container in the centre-left, three deployment targets in a centre-right column, the AI Chatbot box and Vector DB on the right, and Cloud AI APIs at far right. Two centred body paragraphs sit below the diagram, and the page number and NVIDIA logo sit bottom right with a green vertical bar at the right edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram. Shapes and connections: (1) NVIDIA AI Playground rounded light grey box, with a two-way grey arrow down to (2) NVIDIA AI Foundations rounded light grey box, which has a two-way grey arrow down to (3) a DGX Cloud cloud outline at bottom left, containing Microsoft Azure, Google Cloud and ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure logos. (2) has a green arrow pointing right to (4) a dashed grey rounded rectangle labelled LLM Model Container, containing a neural-network icon (nodes and edges, black line art) above a solid green box labelled NVIDIA AI Enterprise. A green arrow runs right from the container to a vertical green bus line at about x 42.5% of slide width, from about y 30% to 64%, with green arrowheads pointing right into three targets: (5) a DGX Cloud cloud outline at top with Microsoft Azure, Google Cloud and ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure logos; (6) the middle label Enterprise On-Prem with DELL, Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Lenovo and vmware logos in a 2 by 2 grid; (7) the label Enterprise SaaS and AI Platforms with Adobe, databricks, gettyimages, Hugging Face, servicenow, snowflake and WPP logos. Grey double-headed arrows join the top DGX Cloud and Enterprise On-Prem and Enterprise SaaS targets to the AI Chatbot box: the top one is an elbow arrow, the middle one a straight horizontal double arrow, and the bottom one an elbow arrow. The AI Chatbot 'RAG' box (light grey rounded rectangle) has a grey double arrow above it to a black line-art person icon (head circle and trapezoid body, no fill), a grey double arrow below it to a black line-art database cylinder icon labelled Vector DB, and a horizontal grey double arrow to the right to a Cloud AI APIs cloud outline. Logos are shown in their own colours: Microsoft Azure four-colour squares, Google Cloud grey wordmark, ORACLE CLOUD red and grey, DELL blue, Hewlett Packard Enterprise green-outline mark with dark grey text, Lenovo red box with white text, vmware grey wordmark, Adobe red, databricks red and grey, gettyimages black, Hugging Face yellow emoji with dark text, servicenow dark grey, snowflake blue, WPP grey-lilac wordmark. NVIDIA logo bottom right in green and black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black and green sans-serif type in the NVIDIA Sans family (a geometric grotesque). Title is NVIDIA Sans Bold, black #000000, about 4.45% of slide height. Subtitle is NVIDIA Sans Regular in NVIDIA green #76B900, about 2.96%. Body text is NVIDIA Sans Regular in black #000000, about 1.98%. Diagram labels are NVIDIA Sans Medium black #000000, about 1.98%. Light grey boxes are #F2F2F2 with thin grey outlines and small corner radius (about 1% of width). The green box is #76B900 with white Medium text and a small corner radius. The AI Chatbot box and cloud outlines have black or grey outlines of about 1 pt. Connector lines are grey #B9B9B9 for the horizontal and vertical arrows and green #76B900 for the main flow arrows, about 1.5 pt with small triangular arrowheads. No shadows or gradients. A green vertical bar sits at the right edge.

**Review notes**

**Text**

**Layout**

Two equal grey panels sit side by side in the lower-middle area of a 16:9 slide, with the left panel spanning about x 4.5–49.2% and the right about x 50.8–95.5%, both from y 23.6% to 82.0%. A centred bold black title sits at the top with a green centred subtitle beneath it, and the page number and NVIDIA logo sit in the bottom-right corner with a thin green bar at the right edge.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a grid of eight outline icons in NVIDIA green (#76B900) in two rows of four. Row 1: LLM (a waveform above the word 'Hello' with a downward arrow), Speech AI (a waveform square linked by arrows to a molecule-like atom square), Recommenders (a pair of pants, a shirt and a sock linked by arrows), Cybersecurity (a shield with an atom-like node network). Row 2: Medical Imaging (a rectangular scan frame with a spine-like graphic and a small device), Video Analytics (a human figure in a bounding-box frame), Route Optimization (a map sheet with a location pin), and a 'More' cell with three black ellipsis dots. Right panel: a dark grey-to-silver metallic sphere (about #4A4F57 with lighter highlights) containing a white-blue network of nodes, labelled NVIDIA AI Enterprise to its right; a black 1px line runs down from the sphere bottom to a horizontal bracket line with two short downward ticks, feeding a green outline cloud icon labelled 'Cloud' (#76B900 outline, no fill) and a green outline server-box icon (rectangle with inner rounded rectangle and side brackets, #76B900 outline). No photographs. The NVIDIA logo (green and black) sits bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background; panels are flat #F2F2F2 with a faint light-grey 1px border. Title is black NVIDIA Sans Bold (sans-serif) at about 4.45% of slide height, subtitle is NVIDIA Sans Regular in green #76B900 at about 2.96%. Panel headings are NVIDIA Sans Medium black at about 2.47%. Body text is NVIDIA Sans Regular black #000000 at about 1.73%, labels at about 1.48%. Shapes have no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Accents are #76B900 green.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 6, 7 and 33 use a stair-step diagram whose labels are tiny and scattered, and slides 4, 14, 16, 18, 19 and 20 carry diagrams or logos at sizes an audience cannot read.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild the stair-step graphics on slides 6, 7 and 33 with readable labels and sizes, and enlarge or cut the detail on slides 4, 14, 17, 19 and 20 so each diagram has one point that can be read.

**Text**

NVIDIA AI Enterprise / Broad and deep ecosystem and distribution to reach every enterprise / GSI & Service Delivery / accenture / Booz | Allen | Hamilton / Capgemini / Deloitte. / Infosys / TCS TATA CONSULTANCY SERVICES / wipro / AI Platforms / databricks / Hugging Face / snowflake / Software Platforms / gettyimages / servicenow / shutterstock / Adobe / WPP / Public Cloud Marketplaces / aws / Google Cloud / ORACLE Cloud Infrastructure / Microsoft Azure / Private Cloud / vmware / Server OEMs / BOXX / CISCO / DELL Technologies / HPE GreenLake / hp / Lenovo / SUPERMICRO / 20

**Layout**

Centered title block at top with the title above a green subtitle, both horizontally centred. Below, a two-column grid: a tall dark-grey left column (GSI & Service Delivery) spanning the full height of the content area, and a right area of 2 columns by 4 rows with a full-width green band across the top of the right area, then two grey panels in the left-right pair (AI Platforms, Software Platforms), then Public Cloud Marketplaces on the left and Private Cloud above Server OEMs on the right. Page number 20 and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom right, with a thin green vertical bar at the far bottom-right edge. Margins are about 4.5% on left and right.

**Visuals**

Six white line-art icons on the green band, left to right: (1) a hospital/medical scanner: a patient table with a bed and a tall medical scanner unit with a plus sign; (2) a factory building with two smokestacks and a sawtooth roof; (3) a telecom tower with radiating signal arcs; (4) an industrial robotic arm on a base; (5) a server rack/data-centre cabinet with stacked units; (6) an outline of a car. Logos are white on dark grey panels: GSI column accenture, Booz Allen Hamilton, Capgemini, Deloitte., Infosys, TCS (Tata Consultancy Services), wipro; AI Platforms databricks, Hugging Face (emoji logo plus wordmark), snowflake; Software Platforms gettyimages, servicenow, shutterstock, Adobe, WPP; Public Cloud aws, Google Cloud, Oracle Cloud Infrastructure, Microsoft Azure; Private Cloud vmware; Server OEMs BOXX, Cisco, Dell Technologies, HPE GreenLake, HP, Lenovo, Supermicro. No charts, photographs or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles black, subtitle and section headings green #76B900. Panels are flat dark grey #5E5E5E with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Logos rendered white. Typeface NVIDIASans (sans-serif) with Bold for title, Regular for subtitle and page number, Medium for section headings. Title about 4.45% of slide height, subtitle 2.96%, section headings 2.47%, page number 1.48%. Green band #76B900. Thin green bar at bottom right edge.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

Driving Strong & Profitable Growth / Revenue ($M) / $26,914 / $26,974 / $11,716 / $10,918 / $16,675 / $20,699 / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 1H FY24 / Operating Income (Non-GAAP, $M) / Operating Margin (Non-GAAP) / $15,000 / $12,000 / $9,000 / $6,000 / $3,000 / $0 / 70% / 60% / 50% / 40% / 30% / $12,690 / $10,828 / $9,040 / $6,803 / $4,407 / $3,735 / 52% / 47% / 41% / 38% / 34% / 34% / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 1H FY24 / 1H FY21 / 1H FY24 / 43 / 42 / 7 / 4 / 4 / 3 / 3 / 1 / 23 / 70 / Gaming / Data Center / ProViz / Auto / OEM & Other / Fiscal year ends in January. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures. Operating margins rounded to the nearest percent. / FY23 financial metrics reflect a $2.2B charge for inventory and related reserves primarily related to Data Center and Gaming. / 21

**Layout**

Single white slide with a centred bold title across the top, above a two-column grid: a large Revenue chart panel on the left (about 45% of width) and, on the right, a stacked pair of boxed charts (operating income and margin on top, two donut charts below). Each right-hand chart sits in its own thin grey-outlined box with a small italic footnote beneath it; a footnote spans the bottom of the right column. Page number '21' and the NVIDIA logo sit bottom-right, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Three charts. (1) Column chart titled 'Revenue ($M)' with six columns, no visible axes or gridlines; value labels above each column: FY19 $11,716; FY20 $10,918; FY21 $16,675; FY22 $26,914; FY23 $26,974; 1H FY24 $20,699. FY23 column is two-tone: bottom segment dark green #598B00 up to about 60% of its height, top segment bright green #76B900; a pale green #CDE5A0 wedge with a black arrow rises from the FY23 column to the 1H FY24 column, which is dark green #598B00. Columns are plain flat bars, bright green #76B900, bottom-aligned on a thin grey baseline. (2) Combo chart with legend at top: green square 'Operating Income (Non-GAAP, $M)' and black line 'Operating Margin (Non-GAAP)'. Left axis $0 to $15,000 in $3,000 steps (USD millions); right axis 30% to 70% in 10% steps. Columns with labels: FY19 $4,407; FY20 $3,735; FY21 $6,803; FY22 $12,690; FY23 $9,040; 1H FY24 $10,828. Margin line with point labels: FY19 38%; FY20 34%; FY21 41%; FY22 47%; FY23 34%; 1H FY24 52%; the line is black, thin, with a black arrow from the FY23 column top to the 1H FY24 column top. Columns are bright green #76B900 with the FY23 and 1H FY24 segments shaded darker #598B00 and pale #CDE5A0. (3) Two donut charts side by side, titled '1H FY21' and '1H FY24', with a shared legend at right: Gaming (bright green #76B900), Data Center (teal #008564), ProViz (purple), Auto (blue), OEM & Other (magenta). 1H FY21 values: Gaming 43, Data Center 42, ProViz 7, Auto 4, OEM & Other 4 (white labels on segments). 1H FY24 values: Gaming 23, Data Center 70, ProViz 3, Auto 3, OEM & Other 1 (white labels). Donut holes are white, segments flat with no outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate look. Title in bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIASans Bold) black #000000, about 4.5% of slide height, centred. Chart titles in medium sans-serif black #000000 about 2.5% of height; body/labels regular sans-serif black #000000 about 1.4-1.7%. Footnotes italic sans-serif grey #8C8C8C about 1.2%. Accent green #76B900, dark green #598B00, teal #008564, pale green #CDE5A0. Panels have thin light grey (#BFBFBF) square-cornered outlines of about 0.75pt, no fill, no shadow. Page number regular sans-serif black. Green accent bar at right edge.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Paired bars and margin lines with a plain headline show gross profit and margin trends in one readable view.
- Review: The deck has a clear route from why accelerated computing, to the generative AI opportunity, products, financials and a market size, and slides 5, 13 and 22 show real craft with a labelled log chart, a plain two-column comparison and a chart paired with a takeaway.
- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

NVIDIA Gross Margins Reflect Value of Acceleration / Accelerated computing requires full-stack and data / center-scale innovation across silicon, systems, / algorithms and applications. / Significant expertise and effort are required, but / application speed-ups can be incredible, resulting / in dramatic cost and time-to-solution savings. / For example, 2 NVIDIA HGX nodes with 16 NVIDIA H100 / GPUs that cost $400K can replace 960 nodes of CPU / servers that cost $10M for the same LLM workload. / NVIDIA chips carry the value of the full-stack, / not just the chip. / Gross Profit (Non-GAAP, $M) Gross Margin (Non-GAAP) / $17,969 / $15,965 / $14,417 / $10,947 / 70% / 67% / 66% / $7,233 / $6,821 / 63% / 62% / 59% / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 1H FY24 / 22 / Cost comparison example based on latest available NVIDIA A100 GPU and Intel CPU inference results in the commercially available category of the MLPerf industry benchmark; includes related infrastructure costs such as networking. / FY23 financial metrics reflect a $2.2B charge for inventory and related reserves primarily related to Data Center and Gaming. / Fiscal year ends in January. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures. Gross margins are rounded to the nearest percent.

**Layout**

Two equal-width bordered panels sit side by side in the content area: a text panel on the left (x 4.5 to 49.2 percent) and a chart panel on the right (x 50.8 to 95.5 percent), both spanning roughly y 23.6 to 82.0 percent. The title is centred horizontally across the top at about y 7 percent. Captions sit under each panel in small grey italic, left-aligned to the panel edges, and the page number and NVIDIA logo sit at bottom right with a green accent bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Chart on the right panel: a combined bar and line chart. Legend across the top of the panel: a small green square followed by 'Gross Profit (Non-GAAP, $M)', then a black line marker followed by 'Gross Margin (Non-GAAP)'. Bars (gross profit, $M, no printed axis values visible because the axis labels are white on white): FY19 $7,233 in #76B900; FY20 $6,821 in #76B900; FY21 $10,947 in #76B900; FY22 $17,969 in #76B900; FY23 $15,965 drawn as two stacked segments, a dark green #598B00 lower section up to about 59 percent height and a bright green #76B900 upper section; 1H FY24 $14,417 in dark green #598B00. Between the FY23 and 1H FY24 bars a pale green #CFE8A0 wedge with a black arrow runs from the top of FY23 to the top of 1H FY24. Margin line (black, thin, about 1 px, with no markers) with printed labels: FY19 62%, FY20 63%, FY21 66%, FY22 67%, FY23 59%, 1H FY24 70%. Category axis labels beneath the baseline: FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 1H FY24. A thin grey horizontal baseline runs under the bars. Hidden white axis labels (invisible on the white background): $0, $6,000, $12,000, $18,000 on the left scale and 55%, 60%, 65%, 70%, 75%, 80% on the right scale. A white '80%' label sits at the top right, outside the visible area. No gridlines. Left panel: text only, no images or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no ornament. Title in NVIDIA Sans Bold (close to a geometric grotesque, sans-serif), black #000000, about 4.45 percent of slide height, centred. Body text in NVIDIA Sans Regular, black #000000, about 2.7 percent of slide height for the main paragraphs and about 2.35 percent for the indented example. Chart labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular, black, about 1.7 percent. Accent colours: bright green #76B900, dark green #598B00, pale green #CFE8A0. Captions in NVIDIA Sans Italic, grey #8C8C8C, about 1.24 percent. Panel borders are thin light grey hairlines (about #BFBFBF, 1 px), square corners, no shadow, no fill. Page number in NVIDIA Sans Regular black. Logo in green and black.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a centred bold title at the top and two side-by-side bordered panels below it: a bar chart panel on the left (about 4.5 to 49.2 percent of width) and a Capital Allocation text panel on the right (about 50.8 to 95.5 percent). Both panels span about 23.6 to 81.9 percent of slide height. Footnotes sit centred under the left panel at about 83 to 85 percent height; the slide number and NVIDIA logo sit bottom right at about 96 percent height, with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled Free Cash Flow (Non-GAAP), vertical bars, no y-axis, no numeric axis scale or units printed beyond the $ values, horizontal baseline in grey about #8C8C8C running from about 7.9 to 45.8 percent of width at about 74.7 percent of height. Categories left to right: FY19 $3.1B (bar about #76B900), FY20 $4.3B (about #76B900), FY21 $4.7B (about #76B900), FY22 $8.0B (about #76B900), FY23 $3.8B (stacked: upper segment about #76B900 and lower segment about #598B00, the lower segment reaching the baseline), 1H FY24 $8.7B (bar about #598B00, dark olive green). Between the FY23 bar and the 1H FY24 bar is a pale green wedge about #CDE6A0 that tapers from the top of the dark FY23 segment up to the top of the 1H FY24 bar. A thin black arrow runs from the top of the dark FY23 segment diagonally up to the top of the 1H FY24 bar, with its arrowhead at the top of that bar. No legend. Values are printed for every bar, so no estimates. Right panel contains no chart, only centred text. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with stylised green eye mark, green about #76B900 and black about #000000.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background (#FFFFFF). Title is bold NVIDIASans-style grotesque sans-serif in black #000000 at about 4.45 percent of slide height. Panel headings are medium weight black #000000 at about 2.47 percent. Section headings in the right panel are medium weight green #76B900 at about 2.47 percent. Body text is regular black #000000 at about 2.22 percent. Chart value labels and category labels are regular black at about 1.73 percent. Footnotes are italic grey #8C8C8C at about 1.24 percent. Panels are outlined rectangles with thin light grey #BFBFBF hairline strokes, square corners, no fill, no shadow. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 with dark green #598B00 for secondary data.

24

**Layout**

Four equal-width columns of roughly 21.8% slide width each, starting at about x 4.5% and spaced about 23% apart, with a centred title at the top (y 7.3%) and a row of photographs at y 23.6% to 58.7%. Each column carries a centred green heading, a percentage line, a bold revenue line, a CAGR line and centred bullet-free description lines beneath the photo. The page number sits bottom right at x 91.3%, followed by the NVIDIA logo at x 93.1% and a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Four rectangular photographs in a row, each sharp-edged and boxed (not full-bleed), same size and aligned on top edge. Column 1, Data Center: a black server rack with stacked gold-grey NVIDIA GPU modules and blue-lit networking cables, shown frontal and filling the frame, lit by even cool studio light, dominant colours black, gold-grey and blue. Column 2, Gaming: a grid of video game key art tiles (Returnal, Sackboy, Dying Light 2, The Witcher 3, Cyberpunk-style yellow art, Minecraft, Fortnite, Plague Tale Requiem) with a laptop in front showing the Portal with RTX logo, and an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090-style graphics card standing to its right; dominant colours dark grey, blue, yellow and green. Column 3, Professional Visualization: a red-and-chrome radio-controlled buggy on a reflective surface with blue and pink light tubes and purple bokeh, shallow depth of field, dominant colours purple, magenta and blue. Column 4, Automotive: a silver Mercedes-Benz concept electric sedan seen front three-quarter, its grille lit with blue LED points and a Mercedes star, set outdoors with trees and a glass building behind, daylight, dominant colours silver grey and green. The photos carry a dark #151617 fill at 61% opacity layered with them per the file measurements. Small NVIDIA logo bottom right at x 93.1%, y 94.8%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, black bold NVIDIA Sans titles and body text, NVIDIA green #76B900 column headings and a green accent bar at the bottom right edge. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans) in bold, medium and regular weights. Title is about 4.45% of slide height, column headings about 2.47%, body text about 1.98%, footer about 1.48%. No shapes, shadows, gradients or rounded corners; photos are plain rectangles with thin edges.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

Data Center / The leading computing platform for AI, HPC & graphics / Revenue ($M) / 51% 5-YR CAGR / Through FY23 / $15,005 / $14,607 / $10,613 / $6,696 / $2,932 / $2,983 / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 1H FY24 / Leader in AI & HPC / #1 in AI training and inference / Used by all hyperscale & major cloud computing / providers and 40,000 enterprises / Powers 74% of the TOP500 supercomputers / Growth Drivers / Rapid AI adoption across industries / Full-stack AI | Software / Three chip strategy — GPU | CPU | DPU / Rising computation requirements for modern AI / Data-center scale innovation / Omniverse / 25

**Layout**

Two equal bordered panels sit side by side below a centred title block: a bar chart panel on the left (about x 4.5% to 49.2%) and a text panel on the right (about x 50.8% to 95.5%), both spanning y 23.6% to 82.0%. The title 'Data Center' is centred at the top (y 7.3%) with a green subtitle centred beneath it. The page number sits bottom right at x 91.3%, with the NVIDIA logo to its right at the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled 'Revenue ($M)', centred at the top of the left panel. Type: column chart with six categories on the x-axis (FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 1H FY24) and no printed y-axis scale or gridlines. Series values labelled above each bar: FY19 $2,932; FY20 $2,983; FY21 $6,696; FY22 $10,613; FY23 $15,005; 1H FY24 $14,607. Units are $M as stated in the title. Bars FY19 to FY22 are solid #76B900. The FY23 bar is stacked: a lower dark green #598B00 segment up to about the level of $10,613 and an upper #76B900 segment to $15,005. Between FY23 and 1H FY24 a pale green #CFE6A2 wedge tapers from the FY23 top-of-dark level up to the top of the 1H FY24 bar, with a thin black arrow line running from the FY23 dark-segment top corner up to the top of the 1H FY24 bar. The 1H FY24 bar is solid #598B00. Callout text at top left of the chart reads '51% 5-YR CAGR / Through FY23'. A thin grey #8C8C8C horizontal baseline runs under all bars. No legend. The right panel is text only, no imagery.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background #FFFFFF with no gradient. Title 'Data Center' is black #000000 bold sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold), about 4.45% of slide height. Subtitle is green #76B900 regular sans-serif, about 2.96%. Panel headings are green #76B900 medium sans-serif at about 2.47%. Body text is black #000000 regular sans-serif at about 2.22%, centred. Chart labels are black regular at about 1.73%. Accent colours: #76B900 bright green, #598B00 dark green, #CFE6A2 pale green. Panels have thin light grey #BFBFBF 1px borders with square corners and no shadow. A thin vertical green bar sits at the far right edge near the bottom.

Single-column title block centred at the top with a bold one-line title and a three-line regular subtitle stacked beneath it. Below sits a large light-grey panel holding a grid of year headers (2021, 2023, 2024, 2025) across the top, a product matrix of rows and columns with green arrows connecting items, and a right-aligned-left text column of row labels at x 63.8%. Page number sits bottom right at the far right of the NVIDIA footer row, followed by the NVIDIA logo and a green vertical bar at the right edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a flow/roadmap matrix with no chart axes. Shapes are plain text labels with no boxes. Connectors are thin green (#76B900) straight arrows with small triangular heads, drawn between labels as follows: A100 right to H100; H100 right to H200; H100 diagonally down-right to L4OS; H200 right to B100; B100 right to X100; upward arrows H200 to GH200 to GH200NVL; B100 up to GB200 up to GB200NVL; X100 up to GX200 up to GX200NVL; downward arrows B100 to B40 and X100 to X40; Quantum row arrows 400G to 800G to 1,600G; Spectrum-X row arrows 400G to 800G to 1,600G. Bold labels mark A100, H100, H200, B100, X100, Quantum and Spectrum-X; all other labels are regular weight. No photographs, icons or chart series. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with stylised green eye mark, at bottom right, placed as a small raster image (1829 by 630 pixels).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with a light grey panel #F2F2F2 and a hairline border about #D9D9D9. Title and subtitle text in black #000000; body and labels black #000000; accent green #76B900 for arrows, logo and the vertical bar at the right edge. Typeface NVIDIASans, a geometric sans-serif: Bold for the title and bold labels, Regular for the subtitle and body. Title about 4.0% of slide height, subtitle and labels about 3.3% and 2.5% of slide height respectively, page number about 1.5%. Panel has square corners, no shadow and no gradient.
