### Slide 27

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip / NVIDIA BlueField-3 / NVIDIA GH200 / 72-Core Grace CPU / 500 GB LPDDR5X / 4 PFLOPS Hopper GPU / 141 GB/5 Tbps HBM3e / 27 / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Single-slide composition on a white field with the title centred across the top, a large centred product photograph of the superchip board occupying the middle, two right- and left-aligned callout labels flanking the image with thin leader lines, and a small page number and NVIDIA logo lockup at the bottom right with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip server board/module viewed from a three-quarter overhead angle, shown as a dark translucent-looking chassis with a black and dark-grey circuit board. Two GH200 modules sit in the lower half, each a square-ish board with a heat-spreader carrying a dark GPU die with a colourful (green, yellow, blue) rendered die image and smaller memory stacks on both sides. Two BlueField-3 DPU cards sit in the upper left and upper right of the chassis, each outlined with a thin white outline stroke tracing the card shape. Lighting is soft and studio-like, the chassis is dark charcoal to black with grey highlights, and the image is not cropped, boxed without border, with no overlay or tint. Leader lines: a thin grey horizontal line runs from the BlueField-3 label leftward to the upper right card; a thin grey horizontal line runs from the GH200 label rightward to the lower left GH200 module.

**Design**

Background pure white. Title 'NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip' in bold black NVIDIASans-style humanist sans-serif, about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Callout headings 'NVIDIA BlueField-3' and 'NVIDIA GH200' in NVIDIASans Medium black about 2.72% of slide height. Callout sub-lines in NVIDIASans Regular black about 2.22% of slide height, right-aligned, line spacing about 3.4% of slide height. Leader lines are thin mid-grey (#8C8C8C), about 0.1% of slide height. Page number in NVIDIASans Regular about 1.48% of slide height, black. Accent: NVIDIA green (#76B900) logo and vertical bar at right edge. No shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients other than the image's own rendering.

**Layout**

Single full-width chart area on a white slide. The title is centred at the top (about 7% from the top) with a green subtitle beneath it. The stacked area chart spans roughly x 7% to 93% and y 29% to 83%, with its y-axis labels at the left and x-axis labels along the bottom. The source lines are centred near the bottom (about 94% to 97%). The page number sits at the bottom right, followed by the NVIDIA logo and a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked area chart with a black line and a green line overlaid, titled 'Addressing the Entire Data Center' with subtitle '$1T+ data center infrastructure installed base'. Y-axis: $0B at the baseline to $250B at the top, labelled only at those two points (no intermediate ticks). X-axis: FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, Q2 FY24 (labelled 'Annualized' beneath the last tick); no numeric values are printed on the series. Stacked bands from bottom to top: Server CPU / Servers band in dark grey #8C8C8C (about 0 to 40% of the plot height at FY20 rising to about 45% at FY23 and about 46% at Q2 FY24, estimated); Network Infrastructure band in light grey #CDCDCD above it; Other Data Center Infrastructure band in very light grey #F2F2F2 at the top (the top edge is about $250B at Q2 FY24, estimated). Black line labelled 'Revenue' sits near the bottom, nearly flat at about $10B to $15B (estimated) across FY20 to FY23 and dipping slightly at Q2 FY24. Green line (#A0E000 approx., lime) labelled 'NVIDIA Data Center' rises from about $2B at FY20 to about $5B at FY22, about $9B at FY23 and about $30B at Q2 FY24 annualized (all estimated). No legend; series are identified by direct labels. No highlighted points. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark in black with green eye mark, at the bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in bold black NVIDIASans-style sans-serif (#000000), about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Subtitle in regular NVIDIASans-style sans-serif in NVIDIA green #76B900, about 2.96% of slide height, centred. Chart labels in regular sans-serif black #000000 about 1.73% to 1.98% of slide height. Source lines in italic grey #8C8C8C about 1.48% of slide height. Area fills are flat with no outlines or shadows; the black line is about 0.3% of slide height thick and the green line is thicker, lime green. A thin vertical green bar (#76B900) sits at the right edge at the bottom.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the log-scale chart with direct labels and the 1000X callout on slide 5, the 960 versus 2 server comparison on slide 13, and the consistent green identity and chart styling across the financial slides 21 to 32.

**Text**

Gaming / GeForce — the world’s largest gaming platform / Revenue ($M) / 10% 5-YR CAGR / Through FY23 / $12,462 / $9,067 / $7,759 / $6,246 / $5,518 / $4,726 / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 1H FY24 / Leader in PC Gaming / Strong #1 market position / 15 of the top 15 most popular GPUs on Steam / Leading performance & innovation / 200M+ gamers on GeForce / Growth Drivers / Rising adoption of NVIDIA RTX in games / Expanding universe of gamers & creators / Gaming laptops & Gen AI on PCs / GeForce NOW Cloud gaming / 29

**Layout**

Centered title "Gaming" with a green centered subtitle at the top of the slide. Below, two equal-height white panels with thin light-grey borders sit side by side: the left panel holds a column chart (about 4.5% to 49.2% of slide width), the right panel holds centered text blocks (about 50.8% to 95.5%). Panels span about 23.6% to 82.0% of slide height. Page number and NVIDIA logo sit at bottom right, with a green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Column chart titled "Revenue ($M)" with no visible y-axis and no gridlines. Categories along the x-axis: FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 1H FY24. Single series of revenue in $M with printed values: FY19 $6,246; FY20 $5,518; FY21 $7,759; FY22 $12,462; FY23 $9,067; 1H FY24 $4,726. Bars are solid #76B900 for FY19 to FY22 and dark green #598B00 for the lower portion of FY23 and for 1H FY24. The FY23 bar has a light green #76B900 upper segment above a dark green #598B00 base (the boundary sits at about 56.9% of slide height). A narrow light green #CDE6A0 column between FY23 and 1H FY24 carries a thin black arrow running from the top-left corner of that column down to the top of the 1H FY24 bar, marking the decline. Thin grey (#8C8C8C) baseline runs under all bars, about 8.0% to 45.8% of slide width at about 74.7% of slide height. Annotation text '10% 5-YR CAGR Through FY23' sits at upper left inside the chart panel. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold black NVIDIASans-style sans-serif (#000000), large, about 4.5% of slide height. Subtitle in regular NVIDIA green #76B900, about 3.0% of slide height. Right-panel section headings in medium-weight green #76B900 about 2.5% of slide height; body text in regular black #000000 about 2.2% of slide height. Chart labels and category labels in regular black about 1.7% of slide height. Panel borders are thin light grey (#BFBFBF) hairlines with square corners, no shadows. Accent colours are NVIDIA green #76B900 and dark green #598B00 with light green #CDE6A0.

**Layout**

Three equal-width white panels sit side by side in a row across the middle of the slide, each with a thin light-grey border and a centred caption beneath it, under a centred bold title at the top. The source line is centred near the bottom, the page number and NVIDIA logo sit at bottom right, and a thin vertical green bar runs down the right edge.

**Visuals**

Three charts. (1) Left panel, column chart titled 'GeForce Gaming Revenue': two green columns, FY20 (short, about 40% of plot height) and FY23 (tall, about 80% of plot height), on a grey horizontal baseline with no y-axis, values or units printed; a black upward arrow sits between the columns labelled '20% CAGR'; to the right a text block '3YR CAGR / ASP 10% / Units 9%'. (2) Centre panel, titled 'Installed Base': two pie charts stacked vertically. Top pie: dark green wedge labelled 'RTX' (left, about 47%, #598B00) and light green wedge (right, about 53%, #76B900), separated by a thin white line; left label '47% RTX'. Bottom pie: small dark green wedge labelled '3060+' (about 20%, #598B00) and large light green wedge (about 80%, #76B900), separated by a thin white line; left label '20% RTX3060+ / Performance'. (3) Right panel, titled '$699+ Cumulative Sell-Through $': line chart with x-axis 'Weeks After Launch' from 0 to 20 (tick labels every week, Calibri, grey-black, no y-axis labels or units printed), three lines all starting at the origin near the same point: NVIDIA Ada (light green, about #8DC400, steepest, reaching the top right near the end), NVIDIA Ampere (dark green, about #4F7F00, middle curve), NVIDIA Turing (dark green, about #4F7F00, lowest curve); series labels sit at the right end of each line. No values are printed; relative heights are estimates. No legend box; line labels act as legend. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, black text with no accent colour besides NVIDIA green #76B900 used for bars, pies, lines and the vertical bar; secondary dark greens #598B00 and #4F7F00. Title is bold NVIDIA Sans, about 4.45% of slide height, black #000000. Panel titles are NVIDIA Sans Medium about 2.47% of height, black. Chart labels and captions are NVIDIA Sans Regular about 1.98% to 2.47% of height, black; captions are Medium at 2.47%. Source line is NVIDIA Sans Italic about 1.48%, grey #8C8C8C. Page number NVIDIA Sans Regular 1.48%, black. Panels have thin light-grey #BFBFBF hairline borders, square corners, no shadows. Bars and pie wedges are flat fills with no outlines except the white pie separator lines. Chart lines are about 2 pt with no markers.

**Layout**

Single-slide layout on a white field with a centred two-line title block at the top (bold title above a green subtitle), then two equal-height boxed panels side by side: a left chart panel (about x 4.5 to 49, y 23.6 to 80.5) and a right text panel (about x 50.8 to 95.5, y 23.6 to 80.5), both with thin light-grey 1px outlines and square corners. Text in the right panel is centred. The slide number 31 sits bottom right at about x 91.3, y 95.9, followed by the NVIDIA logo at about x 93.1, y 94.8, and a thin vertical green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Column chart titled Revenue ($M), with the title centred above the plot. Y-axis has no drawn scale or labels; bar heights are proportional to the printed values. X-axis categories left to right: FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 1H FY24, with a thin grey baseline. Series values: FY19 $1,130 (solid #76B900 bar, about x 9.5 to 12.7, y 54.2 to 74.5); FY20 $1,212 (#76B900, about x 15.9 to 19.0, top about y 52.8); FY21 $1,053 (#76B900, about x 22.1 to 25.3, top about y 55.6); FY22 $2,111 (#76B900, about x 28.4 to 31.6, top about y 36.5, tallest bar); FY23 $1,544 is a stacked bar: a lower dark-olive segment #598B00 (about x 34.7 to 37.9, y 54.6 to 74.5) and an upper bright #76B900 segment (about top y 46.8). Between FY23 and 1H FY24 a pale light-green #CFE6A3 band (about x 37.9 to 41.1, y 54.6 to 62.5) tapers diagonally from the top of the FY23 dark segment down to the top of the 1H FY24 bar, with a thin black arrow line running along its upper edge pointing down-right to the 1H FY24 bar top. 1H FY24 $674 is a dark-olive #598B00 bar (about x 41.1 to 44.2, top about y 62.5). Annotation at top left of the plot reads 11% 5-YR CAGR / Through FY23 in black. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the FY23 stacked split and transition band.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title Professional Visualization in bold NVIDIASans-Bold sans-serif, black #000000, about 4.45% of slide height. Subtitle Workstation graphics in NVIDIASans-Regular, green #76B900, about 2.96%. Panel headings (Revenue ($M), Leader in Workstation Graphics, Growth Drivers) in NVIDIASans-Medium, black #000000 for the chart title and green #76B900 for the two right-panel headings, about 2.47%. Body text in NVIDIASans-Regular, black #000000, about 2.22%. Chart data labels about 1.73%. Panel outlines thin light-grey #BFBFBF, 1px, square corners, no shadows. Accent green #76B900 and dark olive #598B00. No gradients.

**Layout**

Two equal-width bordered panels sit side by side below a centred title block, with the left panel holding a bar chart and the right panel holding text; a full-width light-grey banner runs beneath both panels, and the slide number and NVIDIA logo sit at the bottom right. The title is centred at the top, margins are about 4.5% left and right, and there is no visible footer text beyond the page number.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart titled Revenue ($M) with the annotation 10% 5-YR CAGR Through FY23 at upper left of the plot. Categories along the x-axis: FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 1H FY24. No y-axis, gridlines or axis tick values are shown; the baseline is a thin grey horizontal line. Values printed above bars: FY19 $641, FY20 $700, FY21 $536, FY22 $566, FY23 $903, 1H FY24 $549. FY19 to FY22 are solid bright green bars of heights proportional to their values. FY23 is a stacked bar: a bright green upper segment and a dark olive lower segment, total $903. 1H FY24 is a dark olive bar at $549. Between the FY23 and 1H FY24 bars a pale green wedge with a thin black arrow rising from its lower-left to its upper-right corner, pointing to the top of the 1H FY24 bar, marks the transition. No legend is shown. Highlighted points: the FY23 bar and the 1H FY24 bar in darker olive. Also present: the NVIDIA logo (bottom right) and a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with no gradients or shadows. Title Automotive is bold black NVIDIASans, about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Subtitle is regular bright green #76B900 about 2.96%. Panel headings are medium weight: Revenue ($M) and Growth Drivers in black or green, Leader in Autonomous Driving in green #76B900, about 2.47%. Body text is regular black #000000 about 2.22%, centred. Chart value labels are regular black about 1.73%. Accent green #76B900, dark olive #548A00 for darker bars, pale green #CCE3A0 for the transition wedge. Panels have thin light grey #BFBFBF square-cornered borders about 0.75 pt. Banner is flat #F2F2F2 with no border, and its text is medium black about 2.72%.

**Layout**

A centred bold title sits at the top of the slide, about 7% from the top edge. Below it, five coloured blocks form a staircase rising to the right, with the bottom row of two blocks (Gaming and Data Center Systems) sitting on a shared baseline at about 80% of slide height and right edge at about 95%. Black bold row labels are left-aligned in an open white area to the left of each tier, each label placed at a different horizontal position in a diagonal progression. Page number 33 sits at bottom right with the NVIDIA logo beside it and a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Staircase diagram with five solid filled rectangles, square corners, no outlines, no shadows, no arrows, no axes. Bottom row: a purple block labelled Gaming and $100B (about 16% wide), next to a lime green block labelled Data Center Systems and $300B (about 40% wide) with a small white gap between them. Second tier: a teal block labelled NVIDIA AI Enterprise & DGX Cloud and $150B (about 32% wide), right-aligned to the slide margin. Third tier: a dark green block labelled Autonomous Machines and $300B (about 23% wide), right-aligned. Top tier: a bright blue block labelled Omniverse Enterprise and $150B (about 15% wide), right-aligned. Labels for tiers are in black bold text to the left of each block: Cloud Service Providers & Consumer Internet (bottom left of Gaming), Enterprise (left of NVIDIA AI block), Autonomous Vehicles & Robotics (left of Autonomous Machines), Industrial Digitalization (left of Omniverse). Values: Gaming $100B, Data Center Systems $300B, NVIDIA AI Enterprise & DGX Cloud $150B, Autonomous Machines $300B, Omniverse Enterprise $150B. Small green wavy steam-like icon at bottom left. NVIDIA logo at bottom right. Thin green vertical bar at far right bottom edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. Title and row labels in black #000000 NVIDIA Sans Bold, sans-serif, title about 4.45% of slide height, labels about 1.73%. Block value text in white #FFFFFF NVIDIA Sans Regular about 1.98%, except Data Center Systems in Trebuchet MS about 2.22%. Accent colours: lime #76B900, purple #5D1682, teal #008564, dark green #00644B, blue #0071C5. Shapes are flat with no outline, no shadow, no corner radius. Page number in black NVIDIA Sans Regular about 1.48%.

### Slide 34

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A short bulleted summary recaps the argument in five lines before the market opportunity slide.
- Review: Many headlines are topic labels rather than claims, and the summary on slide 34 is six small lines with no visual structure, so the case has to be recovered by the reader.
- What would change the score: I would replace topic headlines such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' with claim headlines, and make slide 34 a stronger closing page with the key figures visible.

**Text**

Summary / Gen AI is the tipping point for the new computing era / AI is the new software and Accelerated Computing the new hardware / Huge ROI from Gen AI – from new revenue or dramatically lower costs - is driving a powerful new investment cycle / NVIDIA’s accelerated computing platform delivers unmatched performance and TCO savings / Strong revenue, operating profit, and cash flow growth / $1T market opportunity / 34

**Layout**

Single-column centred layout on a white field. The title 'Summary' sits centred horizontally near the top, and six body lines are stacked centred in the middle band with even vertical spacing of about 9 percent of slide height. The NVIDIA logo and green accent bar sit in the bottom-right corner with the slide number to the left of the logo.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no gradients or shadows. Title in NVIDIASans Bold, black #000000, about 4.45 percent of slide height (capitals about 0.7 of that). Body lines in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 2.47 percent of slide height, centred, no bullets. Accent is the NVIDIA green (#76B900) on the logo and a thin vertical green bar at the far right edge. Sans-serif throughout with regular and bold weights.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to / GAAP Financial Measures / 35

**Layout**

Single title slide with no grid. The title sits left-aligned in two lines at about 4.3% from the left edge, vertically centred around 25.8% to 37.4% of the slide height. A thin green vertical bar runs down the left edge from the top to about 36.5% of the height. The page number sits at the bottom right, about 5% from the bottom edge, with a logo box placed just to its right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract illustration covering the whole slide: smooth, glossy, curved green layered ribbons or panels. They sweep diagonally from the upper left, where they fade into white, to the lower right, where they become saturated green and rounded, with dark green shadowed recesses at the far right edge. Soft white highlight lines run along the edges of the layers. The composition is cropped at all four edges, with no text or logo visible within the artwork. A small logo image is placed at the bottom right, but no distinct logo mark is discernible against the green artwork at this scale.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed green-and-white abstract illustration over a solid #FFFFFF fill, fading from white at upper left to green #8CC800 at lower right. White background that fades through pale green into saturated green on the right, created by a full-bleed illustration over a white fill. Title text is black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, a geometric humanist sans-serif, at about 5.6% of slide height for each line, set in a tight two-line block. Page number is black #000000 in NVIDIASans Regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the left bar. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadow or border on the bar. Illustration has soft glossy shading and no visible text.

**Layout**

Title is centred horizontally across the top of the slide with its baseline near 11% of slide height and a margin of about 7% from the top edge. A single full-width table sits below it, spanning from about 6.9% to 93.1% of slide width, with a black header row at about 19.3% to 30.6% and seven fiscal-year groups of two rows each ending near 88% of slide height. The first column is wider and holds the merged year labels; the remaining seven columns are of near-equal width. Three left-aligned italic footnotes sit at the bottom left margin from about 93% to 97% of height, and the page number 37 and NVIDIA logo sit at bottom right, the logo at about 93% to 99% of width.

**Visuals**

A table with eight columns and thirteen rows including the header. Columns: Operating Income and Margin ($ in Millions & Margin Percentage) | Non-GAAP | Acquisition Termination Cost | Acquisition-Related and Other Costs (A) | Stock-Based Compensation (B) | IP-Related Costs | Other (C) | GAAP. Rows are grouped in pairs: the first row of each pair shows dollar values in millions and the second row shows the margin percentage, with the year label merged down the first column. Rows: FY 2019: $4,407 / — / (2) / (557) / (35) / (9) / $3,804; 37.6% / — / — / (4.7) / (0.3) / (0.1) / 32.5%. FY 2020: $3,735 / — / (31) / (844) / (14) / — / $2,846; 34.2% / — / (0.3) / (7.7) / (0.1) / — / 26.1%. FY 2021: $6,803 / — / (836) / (1,397) / (38) / — / $4,532; 40.8% / — / (5.0) / (8.4) / (0.2) / — / 27.2%. FY 2022: $12,690 / — / (636) / (2,004) / (9) / — / $10,041; 47.2% / — / (2.5) / (7.4) / — / — / 37.3%. FY 2023: $9,040 / (1,353) / (674) / (2,710) / (16) / (63) / $4,224; 33.5% / (5.0) / (2.5) / (10.0) / (0.1) / (0.2) / 15.7%. 1H FY 2023: $5,280 / (1,353) / (324) / (1,227) / — / (9) / $2,367; 35.2% / (9.0) / (2.2) / (8.2) / — / — / 15.8%. 1H FY 2024: $10,828 / — / (311) / (1,576) / (10) / 10 / $8,941; 52.3% / — / (1.5) / (7.6) / — / — / 43.2%. Values are in $ millions except percentages; parentheses denote negatives and — denotes nil. The NVIDIA logo at bottom right is a green eye-shaped mark with the black wordmark NVIDIA. A thin vertical green bar sits at the far right edge near the bottom. No charts, photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate data slide on white. Title in heavy black NVIDIASans Bold sans-serif, about 4.45% of slide height, #000000. Table header row is solid black #000000 with white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans Medium text at about 1.73% of slide height, with the bracketed subline at about 1.23%. Body text is black #000000 NVIDIASans Regular at about 1.73% of slide height, and the year labels are NVIDIASans Medium. Table gridlines are thin 1px light grey, approximately #A6A6A6, with no outer border and no fills in the body rows. Footnotes are italic NVIDIASans at about 1.24% of slide height in grey #8C8C8C. Page number is regular black at about 1.48%. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the logo and right-edge bar. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 38

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to GAAP Financial Measures / ($ in Millions) / Free Cash Flow / Purchases Related to Property / and Equipment / and Intangible Assets / Principal Payments / on Property / and Equipment and Intangible Assets / Net Cash / Provided by / Operating Activities / FY 2019 / $3,143 / 600 / — / $3,743 / FY 2020 / $4,272 / 489 / — / $4,761 / FY 2021 / $4,677 / 1,128 / 17 / $5,822 / FY 2022 / $8,049 / 976 / 83 / $9,108 / FY 2023 / $3,750 / 1,833 / 58 / $5,641 / 1H FY 2023 / $2,171 / 794 / 36 / $3,001 / 1H FY 2024 / $8,691 / 537 / 31 / $9,259 / 38

**Layout**

A single full-width table sits below a centred title, with the title at the top of the slide and the table spanning from about 6.9% to 93.1% of the slide width. The table has a black header row of five cells and a first column of row labels, with white body rows separated by thin grey rules and vertical dividers; the slide number 38 and the NVIDIA logo sit at the bottom right, with a green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

One table with five columns and seven body rows plus a header row. Columns: first column ($ in Millions) with row labels; Free Cash Flow; Purchases Related to Property and Equipment and Intangible Assets; Principal Payments on Property and Equipment and Intangible Assets; Net Cash Provided by Operating Activities. Rows: FY 2019 with $3,143, 600, dash, $3,743. FY 2020 with $4,272, 489, dash, $4,761. FY 2021 with $4,677, 1,128, 17, $5,822. FY 2022 with $8,049, 976, 83, $9,108. FY 2023 with $3,750, 1,833, 58, $5,641. 1H FY 2023 with $2,171, 794, 36, $3,001. 1H FY 2024 with $8,691, 537, 31, $9,259. No chart values, legend or highlighted points. Also one NVIDIA logo (green eye mark with NVIDIA wordmark in black) at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient. Title text is black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.45% of slide height, sentence case. Header row fill is solid black #000000 with white #FFFFFF text in NVIDIASans Medium, about 1.73% of slide height, centred. Body text is black #000000; row labels are NVIDIASans Medium and figures are NVIDIASans Regular, both about 1.73% of slide height, centred. Table rules are thin mid-grey #A6A6A6 hairlines, square corners, no shadows. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 on the logo and the bottom right vertical bar. Page number is NVIDIASans Regular about 1.48% of slide height in black.

### Slide 39

**Text**

NVIDIA
39

**Layout**

Full-bleed abstract green-and-white background with the NVIDIA logo as a small mark in the top-left corner, in the same top-left margin position used for the logo, and a small page number near the bottom-right corner. No title, body text or footer is visible, and the upper-left two-thirds of the slide is left open white space.

**Visuals**

Background illustration: a rendered abstract composition of stacked, curved green ribbon-like layers sweeping from the lower-right toward the upper-left. The layers are smooth, glossy and lit from above, with dark green recesses at the right-hand centre where the layers curve inward into a spiral-like opening. Thin pale highlight lines run along the edges of the layers. The upper-left area fades from pure white into pale mint green, and the layers deepen to saturated lime green (about #8DC800) toward the lower right. The image is full-bleed and covers the entire slide. Logo: the NVIDIA logo (green eye-shaped mark with the black NVIDIA wordmark and registered-trademark symbol) at the top-left, placed over the white area. A second small image sits at the bottom-right, roughly 5.5% wide, placed over the green layers; its content is not clearly legible at this size.

**Design**

Background is white (#FFFFFF) in the upper-left fading through pale mint to saturated lime green (about #8DC800) at the right and bottom, with dark green recesses (about #1F5C00) in the illustration. Logo wordmark is black (#000000) and the mark is NVIDIA green (about #76B900). Page number is set in NVIDIASans Regular, black (#000000), with no title or body typography. The look is minimal, with soft glossy 3D surfaces, no shadows on text, no corner radii, and no visible frames or boxes.

- logo at x 2.2, y 3.8, w 22.9, h 14 [#76B900]: NVIDIA logo: green eye-shaped mark with the black NVIDIA wordmark and registered-trademark symbol (Image 1829 by 630 px placed over the white upper-left area; mark is green (about #76B900), wordmark is black (#000000); no box or background behind it.)
